```
May was our biggest month yet.

Multi-book accounting is live â one set of transactions, two sets of books, posting automatically across GAAP, IFRS, or your management accounts. Campfire is now the first ERP with an MCP store, giving Ember live access to Ramp, HubSpot, Stripe, Salesforce, and any hosted MCP server. And Invoice Recovery has leveled up into a full agent â identifying overdue invoices, sending branded reminders, and escalating based on aging.
Emma and Brendan walk through all of it: Ember Skills, fixed asset batch and split linking, CRM field mapping with live preview and AI suggestions, invoice PDF sync to Salesforce, and a few more things the team shipped.

Ready to see Campfire in action? Book a demo â campfire.ai/book-demo
```

```
We're building the modern accounting platform and we want you on the team.
Campfire gives superpowers to accounting and finance teams. We automate the work that slows teams down: transaction categorization, bank reconciliation, revenue recognition, and reporting, so finance teams can stop building spreadsheets and start driving the business.
Why Campfire? $100M raised in 12 weeks. 10x year-over-year growth. Offices in SF, NYC, and London. Real ownership from day one, not a slice of someone else's task. 100% covered medical, dental and vision, equity, unlimited PTO, and daily catered lunch.
```

### 7. Claude Code + MCP for Finance | SupERPower Hour Session 4 | Campfire

**URL:** https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qFD7bjIktK8
**Date:** 2026-04-17
**Views:** 323
**Duration:** 60:16
**Video ID:** qFD7bjIktK8

**Description:**

```
Want to see how Campfire can power your finance team with AI-native tools? Book a demo at campfire.ai/book-demo.

In this session of the Superpower Hour, Campfire founder and CEO John Glasgow is joined by Nigel Glenday, CFO and COO at Masterworks, the platform that turns iconic paintings into investable assets. With $1.2B in assets under management and 500+ artworks in their portfolio, Masterworks has uniquely complex back-office challenges that pushed their finance team to become a true internal skunkworks. Nigel shares how his team has been building Python-driven workflows, automating journal entries, and orchestrating financial operations at scale, well before vibe coding became a mainstream concept.

The session goes deep on how modern finance teams can leverage Claude Code and MCP to build real, scalable workflows, from pulling live data out of Campfire to generating automated AR aging reports, daily CFO summaries, and investor-ready financial models. Nigel demonstrates live how he uses GitHub Actions as lightweight automation triggers, Streamlit for interactive financial dashboards, and YAML config files as the new "assumptions tab," replacing the need to build complex models in Excel from scratch.

Whether you're a CFO, controller, or finance professional curious about where AI fits into your workflow, this session is packed with practical insights: how to deliver context effectively to language models, when to use plan mode before letting Claude take action, how to think about auditability in code versus spreadsheets, and how to upskill a finance team to work at the intersection of accounting and engineering.

Want to see how Campfire can power your finance team with AI-native tools? Book a demo at campfire.ai/book-demo.
```

**Description:**

```
Everyone's talking about AI in Finance. This is where you actually learn to use it. SupERPower Hour is a four-session limited series where modern finance teams can learn how to actually use AI to automate their most manual workflows. Use prompting best practices to build custom apps, automate repetitive tasks, and connect AI directly to your existing finance stack. Whether you're a CFO exploring AI for the first time or an accountant, this series meets you where you are.

Hosted by John Glasgow. A working session covering two tools that make AI repeatable and practical. We'll build a simple finance agent (think fixed asset policy or a custom workflow), then connect Coefficient to pull live data into Google Sheets and automate reporting. Great for anyone living in spreadsheets.
```

```
Close reduced by 4+ days
```

**Description:**

```
Everyone's talking about AI in Finance. This is where you actually learn to use it. SupERPower hour is a four-session limited series where modern finance teams can learn how to actually use AI to automate their most manual workflows. Use prompting best practices to build custom apps, automate repetitive tasks, and connect AI directly to your existing finance stack. Whether you're a CFO exploring AI for the first time or an accountant, this series meets you where you are.

Lay your foundation. Learn how to write effective prompts, then apply them to real finance workflows and scripts. We'll walk through examples likeÂ building board slidesÂ andÂ cash flow forecasting, cover common mistakes to avoid, and introduce the brain dump technique for dramatically better AI outputs.
No coding experience needed.
```

```
Ember Agents are here. Meet the AI workers built specifically for accounting: running continuously in the background and on-demand at close, so your team can focus on the work that actually requires them.
In this video, see how Ember Agents handle the recurring work that's been consuming your team's time:

- Transaction matching and categorization
- AP and AR processing
- Accruals management

- Period-over-period flux analysis
```

```
Campfire Product Demo | AI-Native ERP for High-Growth Companies

See how Campfire automates the accounting work that nobody wants to do: so your team can close faster, report in real time, and scale without adding headcount.

In this walkthrough, Sherry, demos the full platform end-to-end.

What's covered:

Reporting & dashboards: Fully customizable income statements, multi-entity consolidation across columns, multi-currency support, unlimited custom tags/dimensions, and drill-down to any transaction
Budgets vs. actuals: Upload budgets at any level, compare to actuals with dollar and percentage variance in one click
Custom reports: Drag-and-drop report builder (think: pivot tables), custom financial statements, non-GAAP reporting, and scheduled delivery
Ember AI: Ask questions, run flux analysis with transaction-level explanations, upload bills or contracts for AI drafting, build board slides, and create visuals â all with a full audit trail
Close checklist: Built-in close management with standard and custom tasks, including Ember AI tasks that run automatically (accruals, missing entries, journal entry drafts)
Prepaids, fixed assets & bills: Automated amortization and depreciation schedules that roll up into one monthly journal entry to review and approve
Transactions & bank reconciliation: AI categorization that learns from your data over time, approval workflows with dollar-value thresholds
Revenue Automation: GAAP and non-GAAP revenue dashboard, recurring revenue waterfall, ASC 606-compliant recognition for subscription, milestone, and usage-based billing, contract ingestion via HubSpot, Salesforce, or AI PDF upload
Invoicing: Scheduled and one-off invoices, Stripe payment links, automatic follow-ups, sales tax integration

â Book a demo: campfire.ai
```
